Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the computer_vision object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 299. The moduli space has dimension at most 382 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_299(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 299. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^382(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
